The gulfs between stars whisper truths mankind was never meant to comprehend, truths that dwarf our fleeting existence into cosmic insignificance. Glimmers of forgotten epochs, when non-human civilizations carved cyclopean geometries onto worlds long dead, haunt the fringes of our perception. Such forbidden knowledge, unearthed from crumbling tomes and blasphemous monoliths, breeds a profound psychological unraveling, a slow descent into madness as the veil of comforting illusion is torn away. Driven by an insatiable curiosity, humanity claws at these secrets, summoning entities and reawakening powers best left slumbering in the eons-deep abyss. Cults, shrouded in ritual and whispered promises, fester in the shadows, awaiting the return of those Ancient Ones who sleep within the earth, their awakening heralding not enlightenment, but the utter annihilation of sanity and the cold, uncaring reign of cosmic horror.

The truth, glimpsed through shattered sanity, reveals our cosmic insignificance, driving the weak-minded to gibbering madness and the strong to the shadowed embrace of cults and secret societies, praying to slumbering gods beneath the waves. Yet, humanity, driven by its insatiable curiosity, claws at the veil, seeking answers it cannot comprehend, only to unleash horrors best left buried. For in the stygian depths, something stirs: an ancient consciousness, slumbering for eons, now awakened by our folly, ready to reclaim its rightful place in a universe built on madness and despair.


The gulf between human comprehension and cosmic reality yawns like a bottomless, lightless abyss. We, motes of dust clinging to a forgotten rock, dare to seek knowledge best left undisturbed, forbidden secrets whispered from the void. To glimpse their legacy, to truly grasp our infinitesimal place in the grand, uncaring tapestry, is to invite the unraveling of sanity, a psychic collapse into gibbering madness. This truth breeds desperate cults, hidden societies yearning for communion with entities whose very existence defies earthly laws, promises of power masking ultimate annihilation. For humanity's incessant curiosity, its hunger for the unknown, will inevitably unleash horrors slumbering since the dawn of time, horrors that stir in their ancient tombs, stirring from an aeon-long sleep to reclaim a world no longer theirs, yet rightfully so. And we, in our hubris, will have awakened them.
